function ez_isclean( data ) {
    if (typeof data === 'undefined') { data = document.URL; }
    var re = /(([^<>()[\]\\.,;:\s@\"]+(\.[^<>()[\]\\.,;:\s@\"]+)*)|(\".+\"))(@|%2540|%40)((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\])|(([a-zA-Z\-0-9]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}))/;
    var isClean = !re.test(data);
    return isClean;
}

var ez_queue = new Array();
function sort_queue(a, b)
{
  if( a.priority < b.priority ) return -1;
  else if( a.priority == b.priority ) return 0;
  else return 1;
}

            }
        }
  }
}

function in_array(needle, haystack) {
    var length = haystack.length;

<div class="col-xs-12 hidden-sm hidden-md hidden-lg" style="margin-top: 0px"></div>
<h2 style="margin: 0px auto 0 auto; font-size:1.7em"><a class="hover-effect" href="heart-sounds">
<img alt="heart murmurs" class="img-responsive hidden-xs" height="130" src="https://eaCdn.azureedge.net/picts/torsoAnt-locations160.jpg" style="margin: 0 auto 0 auto" width="150"/>Heart Sounds</a></h2>
<p>
                    Learn basic to advanced heart sound auscultation using our courses, reference guides and quizzes. 
                Over 65 abnormalities are covered. <em>Graded quizzes and course completion certificates now available!</em>
</p>
</div>
<div class="col-xs-12 col-sm-6 col-md-4">
<h2 style="margin: 10px auto 0 auto; font-size:1.7em"><a class="hover-effect" href="lung-sounds">

<p>
                    Improve your recognition of lung sounds through our two courses and reference guide. 
                Use our interactive quizzes to evaluate your auscultation skills. <em>Graded quizzes and course completion certificates now available!</em>
</p>
</div>

<p>

                    and for long term retention.
